<rect x="73" y="1628" width="834" height="176" rx="12" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="89" y="1661" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="89" dy="0">L09｜終點出現的琥珀色表示什麼</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="1695" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">入口 M03：水已接近反應完與游離碘顏色</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="1730" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">水消耗新加碘 → 水耗盡後碘累積 → 清澈樣品可見琥珀色</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="1786" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">出口 M04：微過量碘的終點線索</tspan></text>
<rect x="73" y="1818" width="834" height="176" rx="12" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="89" y="1851" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="89" dy="0">L10｜為何雙電極電流在終點上升</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="1885" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">入口 M03：固定極化電壓下的雙鉑電極電流</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="1920" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">終點後 I₂ 與 I⁻ 共存 → 電極氧化還原可維持 → 電流上升</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="1976" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">出口 M04：雙安培終點與正確方向／單位</tspan></text>
<rect x="73" y="2008" width="834" height="176" rx="12" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="89" y="2041" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="89" dy="0">L11｜為何另一種終點讀到電壓下降</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="2075" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">入口 M03：固定小極化電流下的雙鉑電極電壓</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="2110" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">終點後 I₂ 可用 → 維持指定電流較容易 → 所需電壓下降</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="2166" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">出口 M04：雙電位偵測的下降終點</tspan></text>
<rect x="73" y="2198" width="834" height="176" rx="12" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="89" y="2231" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="89" dy="0">L12｜容量式和庫侖式究竟差在哪裡</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="2265" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">入口 M02：碘的供應方式與定量模式</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="2300" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">加已知力價的碘或電解產碘 → 累積體積或電量 → 換算水量</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="2356" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">出口 M03：容量 V（mL）或庫侖 Q（C）</tspan></text>
<text x="79" y="2420" font-size="20" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="79" dy="0">條件：控制電壓或電流不同，終點讀法也不同。</tspan></text>
<rect x="975" y="1510" width="870" height="940" rx="18" fill="#e8e1f2" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="999" y="1554" font-size="29" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="999" dy="0">D｜由已知水量換成百分比</tspan></text>
<text x="999" y="1594" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="999" dy="0">先扣背景，再由結晶水標定力價，最後除以檢品質量。</tspan></text>
<rect x="993" y="1628" width="834" height="176" rx="12" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1009" y="1661" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">L13｜空白與背景水要在哪一步扣掉</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="1695" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">入口 M03：檢品用量、對應空白與測量歷時</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="1730" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">取得同條件背景 → 扣淨消耗量 → 才換算檢品水量</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="1786" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">出口 M04：淨體積／淨電量與正確取樣基準</tspan></text>
<rect x="993" y="1818" width="834" height="176" rx="12" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1009" y="1851" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">L14｜二水合物的 115 mg 為何只有 18 mg 水</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="1885" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">入口 M03：水合物質量、式量與結晶水個數</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="1920" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">質量除式量 → 水合物莫耳量 → 乘結晶水數與水式量</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="1976" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">出口 M04：已知結晶水質量</tspan></text>
<rect x="993" y="2008" width="834" height="176" rx="12" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1009" y="2041" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">L15｜力價為什麼是水的 mg 除以試劑的 mL</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="2075" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">入口 M03：標準水質量與扣空白後試劑體積</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="2110" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">已知水被滴定 → 每 mL 對應多少水 → 得到實際力價</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="2166" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">出口 M04：f（mg H₂O/mL 試劑）</tspan></text>
<rect x="993" y="2198" width="834" height="176" rx="12" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1009" y="2231" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">L16｜求出水的 mg 後，百分比的分母是誰</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="2265" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">入口 M03：已校正水質量與原檢品質量</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="2300" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">水量統一單位 → 除原檢品質量 → 乘 100%</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="2356" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">出口 M04：以原樣品質量為基準的水分%</tspan></text>
<text x="999" y="2420" font-size="20" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="999" dy="0">條件：背景匹配且只扣一次；水與原樣重統一單位。</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="2510" width="870" height="940" rx="18" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="79" y="2554" font-size="29" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="79" dy="0">E｜沿共沸裝置讀下層水</tspan></text>
<text x="79" y="2594" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="79" dy="0">蒸氣上去、凝液下來，水留下、甲苯迴流。</tspan></text>
<rect x="73" y="2628" width="834" height="176" rx="12" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="89" y="2661" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="89" dy="0">L17｜水和甲苯為何能在低於 100 度一起蒸出</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="2695" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">入口 M02：常壓下互不混溶液體與蒸氣壓</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="2730" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">兩相各提供蒸氣 → 蒸氣壓相加達外壓 → 共蒸後冷凝</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="2786" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">出口 M03：可收集的水／溶劑凝液</tspan></text>
<rect x="73" y="2818" width="834" height="176" rx="12" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="89" y="2851" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="89" dy="0">L18｜蒸氣、凝液和甲苯迴流各走哪裡</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="2885" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">入口 M02：裝置 A–E 的真實連通與冷凝條件</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="2920" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">A 蒸氣經 D 到 B／C → C 冷凝流至 E → 水留 E、甲苯回 A</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="2976" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">出口 M03：留在刻度接收管的水</tspan></text>
<rect x="73" y="3008" width="834" height="176" rx="12" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="89" y="3041" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="89" dy="0">L19｜要讀下層水，為何還要先飽和甲苯與冷卻</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="3075" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">入口 M03：水／甲苯密度、溶解背景與接收管液麵</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="3110" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">減少溶劑吸水 → 冷卻並讓水滴聚集分層 → 讀下層淨水體積</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="3166" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">出口 M04：室溫且含背景校正的水 mL</tspan></text>
<rect x="73" y="3198" width="834" height="176" rx="12" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="89" y="3231" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="89" dy="0">L20｜接收 2 到 4 mL 水，如何決定取樣量</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="3265" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">入口 M01：預估水分、接收目標與水密度</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="3300" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">水分率乘樣重 → 水質量除密度 → 選合適樣重</tspan></text>
<text x="89" y="3356" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="89" dy="0">出口 M02：可讀數 mL 水的代表性樣品量</tspan></text>
<text x="79" y="3420" font-size="20" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="79" dy="0">條件：回收完全、室溫分層，讀下層淨水體積。</tspan></text>
<rect x="975" y="2510" width="870" height="940" rx="18" fill="#f5dfe2" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="999" y="2554" font-size="29" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="999" dy="0">F｜檢查減重與結果是否可信</tspan></text>
<text x="999" y="2594" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="999" dy="0">釐清失去的是什麼，回查具體操作，再認識自動化。</tspan></text>
<rect x="993" y="2628" width="834" height="176" rx="12" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1009" y="2661" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">L21｜重量法如何排除稱量瓶本身的質量</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="2695" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">入口 M03：空瓶、濕樣加瓶、乾樣加瓶三次稱量</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="2730" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">先求原樣重 → 求乾燥質量差 → 除原樣重</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="2786" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">出口 M04：乾燥減重百分比</tspan></text>
<rect x="993" y="2818" width="834" height="176" rx="12" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1009" y="2851" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">L22｜乾燥多久和恆量判準能不能當固定常數</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="2885" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">入口 M02：規定溫度、時間、冷卻與允許重量差</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="2920" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">按規定乾燥 → 乾燥器冷卻稱量 → 按該法差值判停</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="2976" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">出口 M03：符合該方法的終質量</tspan></text>
<rect x="993" y="3008" width="834" height="176" rx="12" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1009" y="3041" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">L23｜結果偏高或偏低，如何回到真正出錯的一站</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="3075" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">入口 M04：結果、平行測定、空白與回收證據</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="3110" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">列候選原因 → 用空白／回收／讀數核對 → 修正對應站</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="3166" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">出口 M02：經修正後重測的可靠結果</tspan></text>
<rect x="993" y="3198" width="834" height="176" rx="12" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1009" y="3231" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">L24｜自動滴定和流動注入是否是同一種方法</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="3265" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">入口 M02：控制訊號與是否有載流注入的配置</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="3300" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">辨回授控制或流動注入 → 追化學／檢測步驟 → 核對應用範圍</tspan></text>
<text x="1009" y="3356" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1009" dy="0">出口 M03：有方法條件的自動化讀數</tspan></text>
<text x="999" y="3420" font-size="20" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="999" dy="0">條件：依該法乾燥判準；偏差須用另一證據區分。</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="3520" width="1790" height="245" rx="18" fill="#eeeade" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="77" y="3560" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="77" dy="0">把一條小循環交回大循環：L15 → L16</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="3609.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">M03 標準水 18 mg、試劑 10.0 mL → L15 每 mL 為 1.80 mg → M04 有效力價。</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="3655.9500000000003" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">另次樣品淨體積×此力價 → L16 水 mg／原樣 mg → M04 水分%；兩次量測不能混作同一筆。</tspan></text>
<text x="55" y="3856" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="55" dy="0">全部 24 條 L 各有一個主要位置。A–F 是問題分組；M01–M04 才是分析站。</tspan></text>
</g>
</svg>

</details>

<details>
<summary>展開圖 3：全章方法分類與大小循環對照圖</summary>

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1900" height="2660" viewBox="0 0 1900 2660" role="img" aria-labelledby="wat3_g3_title wat3_g3_desc">
<title id="wat3_g3_title">水分測定法｜方法分類與大小循環對照</title>
<desc id="wat3_g3_desc">水分測定法v3.0，原生可編輯向量圖。M01先定問題、M02準備與測量、M03讀訊號、M04核對結論。深灰實線為資訊或具條件的因果；紅色虛線為分析者遇到不符時的回查，非分子物理回饋。</desc>
<defs><marker id="wat3_g3_arrow" viewBox="0 0 12 12" refX="10" refY="6" markerWidth="10" markerHeight="10" orient="auto-start-reverse"><path d="M1,1 L11,6 L1,11 Z" fill="#61717a"/></marker><marker id="wat3_g3_red" viewBox="0 0 12 12" refX="10" refY="6" markerWidth="10" markerHeight="10" orient="auto-start-reverse"><path d="M1,1 L11,6 L1,11 Z" fill="#b72d4a"/></marker></defs>
<rect width="1900" height="2660" fill="#f8f6ee"/>
<g font-family="Droid Sans Fallback, Noto Sans CJK TC, PingFang TC, Microsoft JhengHei, sans-serif" fill="#24343f">
<text x="55" y="78" font-size="38" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="55" dy="0">水分測定法｜方法分類與大小循環對照</tspan></text>
<text x="55" y="126" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="55" dy="0">水分測定法 v3.0｜先看用途與條件，再沿入口 → 短鏈 → 出口判讀。</tspan></text>
<text x="55" y="161" font-size="20" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="55" dy="0">KF：費氏水分測定；LOD：乾燥減重；f：mg 水／mL 試劑。灰線資訊向下遊；紅虛線分析者回查。</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="180" width="420" height="77" rx="18" fill="#eeeade" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="75" y="226" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="75" dy="0">M01｜先定問題</tspan></text>
<path d="M475,219 L502,219" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g3_arrow)"/>
<rect x="510" y="180" width="420" height="77" rx="18" fill="#eeeade" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="530" y="226" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="530" dy="0">M02｜準備與測量</tspan></text>
<path d="M930,219 L957,219" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g3_arrow)"/>
<rect x="965" y="180" width="420" height="77" rx="18" fill="#eeeade" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="985" y="226" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="985" dy="0">M03｜讀訊號</tspan></text>
<path d="M1385,219 L1412,219" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g3_arrow)"/>
<rect x="1420" y="180" width="420" height="77" rx="18" fill="#eeeade" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1440" y="226" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1440" dy="0">M04｜核對結論</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="310" width="870" height="675" rx="18" fill="#d9eeea" stroke="#286c68" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="79" y="358" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="79" dy="0">容量式 KF｜水分反應定量模式</tspan></text>
<rect x="79" y="395" width="822" height="117" rx="12" fill="white" stroke="#286c68" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="97" y="426" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="97" dy="0">M01／P1</tspan></text>
<text x="97" y="460" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="97" dy="0">液體、可溶／可萃取固體或合適轉移的水；定量水。</tspan></text>
<path d="M490,512 L490,527" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g3_arrow)"/>
<rect x="79" y="530" width="822" height="117" rx="12" fill="white" stroke="#286c68" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="97" y="561" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="97" dy="0">M02／P2</tspan></text>
<text x="97" y="595" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="97" dy="0">有效 KF 介質、碘對水 1:1；已標定力價、控制背景和副反應。</tspan></text>
<path d="M490,647 L490,662" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g3_arrow)"/>
<rect x="79" y="665" width="822" height="117" rx="12" fill="white" stroke="#286c68" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="97" y="696" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="97" dy="0">M03／P3</tspan></text>
<text x="97" y="730" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="97" dy="0">密閉滴定池、攪拌、含碘滴定液與終點電極；讀 V_net（mL），不是用 µA </tspan><tspan x="97" dy="33.35">算含量。</tspan></text>
<path d="M490,782 L490,797" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g3_arrow)"/>
<rect x="79" y="800" width="822" height="117" rx="12" fill="white" stroke="#286c68" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="97" y="831" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="97" dy="0">M04／P4</tspan></text>
<text x="97" y="865" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="97" dy="0">m_water＝fV_net，再除原樣重；未完全萃取、產水／耗碘與空白影響結果。</tspan></text>
<text x="79" y="960" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="79" dy="0">接點：L12、L13、L15、L16</tspan></text>
<rect x="975" y="310" width="870" height="675" rx="18" fill="#dcecdc" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="999" y="358" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="999" dy="0">庫侖式 KF｜水分反應定量模式</tspan></text>
<rect x="999" y="395" width="822" height="117" rx="12" fill="white" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1017" y="426" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1017" dy="0">M01／P1</tspan></text>
<text x="1017" y="460" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1017" dy="0">適合微量水及適當進樣／釋水的檢品；定量水。</tspan></text>
<path d="M1410,512 L1410,527" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g3_arrow)"/>
<rect x="999" y="530" width="822" height="117" rx="12" fill="white" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1017" y="561" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1017" dy="0">M02／P2</tspan></text>
<text x="1017" y="595" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1017" dy="0">陽極 2I⁻→I₂+2e⁻；有效電流效率、無干擾，以總電量計量。</tspan></text>
<path d="M1410,647 L1410,662" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g3_arrow)"/>
<rect x="999" y="665" width="822" height="117" rx="12" fill="white" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1017" y="696" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1017" dy="0">M03／P3</tspan></text>
<text x="1017" y="730" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1017" dy="0">密閉池、產生電極與獨立終點指示電極；讀 Q_net（C），終點電壓另讀。</tspan></text>
<path d="M1410,782 L1410,797" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g3_arrow)"/>
<rect x="999" y="800" width="822" height="117" rx="12" fill="white" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1017" y="831" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1017" dy="0">M04／P4</tspan></text>
<text x="1017" y="865" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1017" dy="0">Q_net/(2F_c) 得水的 mol，再乘 18 g/mol 求水質量；背景、效率與副反應須</tspan><tspan x="1017" dy="33.35">核對。</tspan></text>
<text x="999" y="960" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="999" dy="0">接點：L12、L13、L16</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="1040" width="870" height="675" rx="18" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="79" y="1088" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="79" dy="0">甲苯蒸餾／共沸法｜分離後體積定量方法</tspan></text>
<rect x="79" y="1125" width="822" height="117" rx="12" fill="white" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="97" y="1156" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="97" dy="0">M01／P1</tspan></text>
<text x="97" y="1190" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="97" dy="0">水量能收集數 mL 的代表性生藥等；按指定方法定量水。</tspan></text>
<path d="M490,1242 L490,1257" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g3_arrow)"/>
<rect x="79" y="1260" width="822" height="117" rx="12" fill="white" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="97" y="1291" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="97" dy="0">M02／P2</tspan></text>
<text x="97" y="1325" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="97" dy="0">水與不混溶溶劑共蒸後冷凝分層；預飽和或預蒸餾背景、回收完整。</tspan></text>
<path d="M490,1377 L490,1392" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g3_arrow)"/>
<rect x="79" y="1395" width="822" height="117" rx="12" fill="white" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="97" y="1426" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="97" dy="0">M03／P3</tspan></text>
<text x="97" y="1460" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="97" dy="0">燒瓶、蒸氣連線、迴流冷凝與刻度接收段；讀下層淨水體積（mL）。</tspan></text>
<path d="M490,1512 L490,1527" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g3_arrow)"/>
<rect x="79" y="1530" width="822" height="117" rx="12" fill="white" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="97" y="1561" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="97" dy="0">M04／P4</tspan></text>
<text x="97" y="1595" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="97" dy="0">m_water≈ρV_net，再除原樣重；壁上水滴、未分層、背景水與回收不完全</tspan><tspan x="97" dy="33.35">會影響。</tspan></text>
<text x="79" y="1690" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="79" dy="0">接點：L17、L18、L19、L20</tspan></text>
<rect x="975" y="1040" width="870" height="675" rx="18" fill="#f5dfe2" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="999" y="1088" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="999" dy="0">重量法／乾燥減重｜指定乾燥條件下質量損失</tspan></text>
<rect x="999" y="1125" width="822" height="117" rx="12" fill="white" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1017" y="1156" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1017" dy="0">M01／P1</tspan></text>
<text x="1017" y="1190" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1017" dy="0">化學藥品或生藥，測規定條件下 LOD；要當水分須先驗證專一性。</tspan></text>
<path d="M1410,1242 L1410,1257" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g3_arrow)"/>
<rect x="999" y="1260" width="822" height="117" rx="12" fill="white" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1017" y="1291" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1017" dy="0">M02／P2</tspan></text>
<text x="1017" y="1325" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1017" dy="0">按規定溫度／時間／壓力或乾燥方式，冷卻稱量到規定終點。</tspan></text>
<path d="M1410,1377 L1410,1392" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g3_arrow)"/>
<rect x="999" y="1395" width="822" height="117" rx="12" fill="white" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1017" y="1426" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1017" dy="0">M03／P3</tspan></text>
<text x="1017" y="1460" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1017" dy="0">乾燥稱量瓶、天平、烘箱／乾燥器；m₀、m₁、m₂（g）。</tspan></text>
<path d="M1410,1512 L1410,1527" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g3_arrow)"/>
<rect x="999" y="1530" width="822" height="117" rx="12" fill="white" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1017" y="1561" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1017" dy="0">M04／P4</tspan></text>
<text x="1017" y="1595" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1017" dy="0">LOD%＝(m₁−m₂)/(m₁−m₀)×100%；其他揮發損失、分解或吸濕影響。</tspan></text>
<text x="999" y="1690" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="999" dy="0">接點：L01、L21、L22</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="1790" width="870" height="290" rx="18" fill="#dcecdc" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="77" y="1830" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="77" dy="0">供碘／計量模式：平行分類</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="1879.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">容量式：含碘液由滴定管加入，水量由 f×mL。</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="1925.95" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">庫侖式：I⁻ 在產生電極氧化，水量由累積 C。</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="1972.75" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">容量／庫侖回答「如何供碘、量多少水」。</tspan></text>
<rect x="975" y="1790" width="870" height="290" rx="18" fill="#e8e1f2" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="997" y="1830" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="997" dy="0">終點指示：另一條分類軸</tspan></text>
<text x="997" y="1879.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="997" dy="0">目視：持續琥珀色；有色背景可能遮蔽。</tspan></text>
<text x="997" y="1925.95" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="997" dy="0">雙安培：固定電壓 → 讀電流上升。</tspan></text>
<text x="997" y="1972.75" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="997" dy="0">雙電位：固定小電流 → 讀電壓下降。</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="2140" width="1790" height="370" rx="18" fill="#eeeade" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="77" y="2180" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="77" dy="0">題目辨識對照｜不是本章四個核心定量配置</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="2229.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">露點法：凝露溫度；NMR：核磁共振；一般 LC：層析配置；FIA：載流注入／檢測。不可只由名稱推成相同水分法。</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="2275.9500000000003" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">Kjeldahl（凱氏）：氮；Fehling（斐林）：還原糖；Volhard（佛哈）：銀量反滴定常測鹵離子。Q11 的試劑不是這三組。</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="2322.7500000000005" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">argentimetric／compleximetric／iodometric 是反應類別；amperometric 是電流測量方式。Q13 問的是後者。</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="2369.5500000000006" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">原分類校正：目視 ≠ dead-stop；電壓下降 ≠ 庫侖量；LOD ≠ 未驗證的水分。</tspan></text>
<text x="55" y="2586" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="55" dy="0">F_c＝96485 C/mol；水的 M≈18 g/mol。同一四站容納不同方法；分類框表示身分，框內箭頭才表示順序。</tspan></text>
</g>
</svg>

</details>

<details>
<summary>展開圖 4：儀器與操作考點圖</summary>

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1900" height="3440" viewBox="0 0 1900 3440" role="img" aria-labelledby="wat3_g4_title wat3_g4_desc">
<title id="wat3_g4_title">水分測定法｜儀器與操作考點</title>
<desc id="wat3_g4_desc">水分測定法v3.0，原生可編輯向量圖。M01先定問題、M02準備與測量、M03讀訊號、M04核對結論。深灰實線為資訊或具條件的因果；紅色虛線為分析者遇到不符時的回查，非分子物理回饋。</desc>
<defs><marker id="wat3_g4_arrow" viewBox="0 0 12 12" refX="10" refY="6" markerWidth="10" markerHeight="10" orient="auto-start-reverse"><path d="M1,1 L11,6 L1,11 Z" fill="#61717a"/></marker><marker id="wat3_g4_red" viewBox="0 0 12 12" refX="10" refY="6" markerWidth="10" markerHeight="10" orient="auto-start-reverse"><path d="M1,1 L11,6 L1,11 Z" fill="#b72d4a"/></marker></defs>
<rect width="1900" height="3440" fill="#f8f6ee"/>
<g font-family="Droid Sans Fallback, Noto Sans CJK TC, PingFang TC, Microsoft JhengHei, sans-serif" fill="#24343f">
<text x="55" y="78" font-size="38" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="55" dy="0">水分測定法｜儀器與操作考點</tspan></text>
<text x="55" y="126" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="55" dy="0">水分測定法 v3.0｜先看用途與條件，再沿入口 → 短鏈 → 出口判讀。</tspan></text>
<text x="55" y="161" font-size="20" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="55" dy="0">KF：費氏水分測定；LOD：乾燥減重；f：mg 水／mL 試劑。灰線資訊向下遊；紅虛線分析者回查。</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="180" width="420" height="77" rx="18" fill="#eeeade" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="75" y="226" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="75" dy="0">M01｜先定問題</tspan></text>
<path d="M475,219 L502,219" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g4_arrow)"/>
<rect x="510" y="180" width="420" height="77" rx="18" fill="#eeeade" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="530" y="226" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="530" dy="0">M02｜準備與測量</tspan></text>
<path d="M930,219 L957,219" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g4_arrow)"/>
<rect x="965" y="180" width="420" height="77" rx="18" fill="#eeeade" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="985" y="226" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="985" dy="0">M03｜讀訊號</tspan></text>
<path d="M1385,219 L1412,219" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g4_arrow)"/>
<rect x="1420" y="180" width="420" height="77" rx="18" fill="#eeeade" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1440" y="226" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1440" dy="0">M04｜核對結論</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="310" width="1790" height="920" rx="18" fill="#dcecdc" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="80" y="355" font-size="29" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="80" dy="0">KF：容量或庫侖擇一供碘，終點電極另接｜功能示意、不按比例</tspan></text>
<rect x="85" y="420" width="440" height="250" rx="18" fill="#fff1c8" stroke="#9e771c" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="107" y="460" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="107" dy="0">容量式供碘</tspan></text>
<text x="107" y="509.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="107" dy="0">標定含碘液 → 滴定管 → 池內。</tspan></text>
<text x="107" y="555.9499999999999" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="107" dy="0">累積淨 V（mL）×f → 水 mg。</tspan></text>
<rect x="620" y="530" width="570" height="425" rx="30" fill="white" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="2"/>
<rect x="630" y="690" width="550" height="250" rx="15" fill="#d9eeea" stroke="#d9eeea" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="620" y="445" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="620" dy="0">密閉池：有效醇／鹼／SO₂＋檢品</tspan></text>
<text x="650" y="900" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="650" dy="0">攪拌；先處理背景，防止環境進水。</tspan></text>
<path d="M525,550 L580,550 L580,625 L850,625 L850,750" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g4_arrow)"/>
<text x="635" y="488" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="635" dy="0">檢品由密閉進樣口加入 ↓</tspan></text>
<path d="M705,595 L705,810" fill="none" stroke="#286c68" stroke-width="7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M735,595 L735,810" fill="none" stroke="#286c68" stroke-width="7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M1015,595 L1015,810" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M1075,595 L1075,810" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<text x="660" y="846" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="660" dy="0">雙 Pt 指示電極</tspan></text>
<text x="957" y="846" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="957" dy="0">產生電極</tspan></text>
<rect x="1315" y="420" width="500" height="300" rx="18" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1337" y="460" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1337" dy="0">庫侖式產碘支路</tspan></text>
<text x="1337" y="509.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1337" dy="0">產生電源 → 產生電極。</tspan></text>
<text x="1337" y="555.9499999999999" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1337" dy="0">陽極：2I⁻ → I₂＋2e⁻。</tspan></text>
<text x="1337" y="602.7499999999999" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1337" dy="0">累積產碘 Q（C）→ 水量。</tspan></text>
<path d="M1315,525 L1250,525 L1250,590 L1015,590" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g4_arrow)"/>
<path d="M1075,580 L1265,580 L1265,675 L1315,675" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g4_arrow)"/>
<rect x="1315" y="775" width="500" height="285" rx="18" fill="#d9eeea" stroke="#286c68" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1337" y="815" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1337" dy="0">終點指示支路</tspan></text>
<text x="1337" y="864.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1337" dy="0">雙 Pt：固定電壓讀 I_det ↑，或固定小電流</tspan><tspan x="1337" dy="34.8">讀 U ↓。</tspan></text>
<text x="1337" y="945.75" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1337" dy="0">只判何時停；µA／mV 不直接當水質量。</tspan></text>
<path d="M705,600 L705,515 L1225,515 L1225,825 L1315,825" fill="none" stroke="#286c68" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g4_arrow)"/>
<path d="M1315,1015 L735,1015 L735,820" fill="none" stroke="#286c68" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g4_arrow)"/>
<text x="85" y="1103" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="85" dy="0">圖例：藍＝產碘電路；綠＝終點指示電路；灰＝容量式加液。此為模式並列功能圖，不是同時雙供碘操作。</tspan></text>
<text x="85" y="1154" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="85" dy="0">M02 有效反應／配置 → L09–L12 → M03 終點與 V 或 Q 分開 → M04 換算／核對。</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="1280" width="1790" height="1260" rx="18" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="80" y="1326" font-size="29" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="80" dy="0">共沸裝置：A–E 標號與甲苯迴流｜連通示意、不按比例</tspan></text>
<circle cx="345" cy="2130" r="142" fill="white"/>
<circle cx="345" cy="2130" r="142" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="5"/>
<rect x="313" y="1930" width="64" height="95" rx="0" fill="white" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<rect x="220" y="2290" width="250" height="35" rx="8" fill="#eeeade" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="303" y="2148" font-size="36" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="303" dy="0">A</tspan></text>
<text x="100" y="2050" font-size="25" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="100" dy="0">檢品＋甲苯</tspan></text>
<text x="218" y="2360" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="218" dy="0">控溫加熱</tspan></text>
<path d="M323,1930 L323,1760 L805,1650 L940,1650" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M367,1930 L367,1793 L814,1694 L940,1694" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<text x="470" y="1700" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="470" dy="0">D 連線管（適當保溫）</tspan></text>
<text x="480" y="1810" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="480" dy="0">蒸氣 → 冷凝區</tspan></text>
<text x="470" y="1855" font-size="23" fill="#9e771c" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="470" dy="0">← 上層甲苯回燒瓶</tspan></text>
<rect x="940" y="1570" width="170" height="350" rx="6" fill="white" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<rect x="980" y="1920" width="90" height="230" rx="5" fill="white" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<rect x="946" y="1683" width="158" height="231" rx="0" fill="#fff1c8" stroke="#fff1c8" stroke-width="2"/>
<rect x="986" y="1920" width="78" height="77" rx="0" fill="#fff1c8" stroke="#fff1c8" stroke-width="2"/>
<rect x="986" y="1997" width="78" height="147" rx="0" fill="#b9d7ed" stroke="#b9d7ed" stroke-width="2"/>
<path d="M986,1997 L1064,1997" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<rect x="945" y="1400" width="160" height="170" rx="8" fill="white" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<path d="M345,1875 L345,1780 L825,1670 L935,1670 L980,1670 L980,1550" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g4_arrow)"/>
<path d="M940,1688 L820,1688 L355,1790 L355,1910" fill="none" stroke="#9e771c" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g4_arrow)"/>
<path d="M995,1400 L995,1570" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M1055,1400 L1055,1570" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M999,1425 L1051,1435" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M999,1450 L1051,1460" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M999,1475 L1051,1485" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M999,1500 L1051,1510" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M999,1525 L1051,1535" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M1105,1535 L1185,1535" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M945,1435 L865,1435" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M1220,1535 L1120,1535" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g4_arrow)"/>
<path d="M920,1435 L820,1435" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g4_arrow)"/>
<text x="1140" y="1497" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1140" dy="0">冷卻水：下口進</tspan></text>
<text x="700" y="1380" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="700" dy="0">上口出（冷卻套）</tspan></text>
<path d="M1030,1510 L1030,1970" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g4_arrow)"/>
<path d="M1070,1940 L1080,1940" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M1070,1960 L1088,1960" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M1070,1980 L1080,1980" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M1070,2000 L1088,2000" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M1070,2020 L1080,2020" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M1070,2040 L1088,2040" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M1070,2060 L1080,2060" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M1070,2080 L1088,2080" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M1070,2100 L1080,2100" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M1070,2120 L1088,2120" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<text x="1250" y="1435" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1250" dy="0">C｜迴流冷凝管</tspan></text>
<text x="1250" y="1590" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1250" dy="0">B｜圓筒管／蒸氣與接收部</tspan></text>
<text x="1250" y="1640" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1250" dy="0">凝液向下；蒸氣向上。</tspan></text>
<text x="1250" y="1730" font-size="24" fill="#9e771c" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1250" dy="0">甲苯上層 → 達迴流口 → 經 D 返回 A。</tspan></text>
<text x="1250" y="1860" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1250" dy="0">E｜刻度接收管</tspan></text>
<text x="1250" y="1910" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1250" dy="0">水在下層累積；只讀淨水體積。</tspan></text>
<text x="1250" y="2020" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1250" dy="0">甲苯密度約 0.87；水約 1 g/mL。</tspan></text>
<text x="1250" y="2080" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1250" dy="0">刻度與液麵為示意，不是實測。</tspan></text>
<text x="90" y="2430" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="90" dy="0">M02 共蒸／冷凝 → L18–L19 → M03 下層水 mL → M04 密度換水量。</tspan></text>
<text x="90" y="2475" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="90" dy="0">操作：預飽和或預蒸餾校正；冷卻、移下壁上水滴、靜置分層，再讀液麵。</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="2600" width="1790" height="365" rx="18" fill="#f5dfe2" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="80" y="2647" font-size="29" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="80" dy="0">重量法：乾燥與稱量的真實操作順序</tspan></text>
<rect x="80" y="2700" width="320" height="155" rx="18" fill="white" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="102" y="2740" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="102" dy="0">空瓶乾燥稱量</tspan></text>
<text x="102" y="2789.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="102" dy="0">m₀（g）</tspan></text>
<path d="M400,2778 L422,2778" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g4_arrow)"/>
<rect x="430" y="2700" width="320" height="155" rx="18" fill="white" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="452" y="2740" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="452" dy="0">加入原樣稱量</tspan></text>
<text x="452" y="2789.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="452" dy="0">m₁（g）</tspan></text>
<path d="M750,2778 L772,2778" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g4_arrow)"/>
<rect x="780" y="2700" width="320" height="155" rx="18" fill="white" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="802" y="2740" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="802" dy="0">按規定乾燥</tspan></text>
<text x="802" y="2789.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="802" dy="0">溫度／時間依方法</tspan></text>
<path d="M1100,2778 L1122,2778" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g4_arrow)"/>
<rect x="1130" y="2700" width="320" height="155" rx="18" fill="white" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1152" y="2740" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1152" dy="0">乾燥器冷卻</tspan></text>
<text x="1152" y="2789.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1152" dy="0">避免熱氣流與再吸濕</tspan></text>
<path d="M1450,2778 L1472,2778" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g4_arrow)"/>
<rect x="1480" y="2700" width="320" height="155" rx="18" fill="white" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1502" y="2740" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1502" dy="0">稱終重／判停</tspan></text>
<text x="1502" y="2789.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1502" dy="0">m₂（g）與指定差限</tspan></text>
<text x="80" y="2920" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="80" dy="0">M02 有效乾燥 → L21–L22 → M03 三個質量 → M04 LOD；未驗證時不把全部減重稱為水。</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="3030" width="1790" height="290" rx="18" fill="#eeeade" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="77" y="3070" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="77" dy="0">操作不符 → 具體回查</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="3119.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">KF 背景持續上升 → 查密封／溶劑／漂移（M02）；體積與電量混用 → 重查模式與讀數（M03）。</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="3165.9500000000003" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">蒸餾壁上仍有水滴或未分層 → 完成匯水／冷卻（M02）；讀上層 → 重讀下層（M03）。</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="3212.7500000000005" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">乾重再吸濕或未冷卻 → 修正冷卻與稱量（M02）；把 LOD 當專一水分 → 重查證據／方法（M01／M04）。</tspan></text>
<text x="55" y="3366" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="55" dy="0">灰／藍／綠實線在硬體區依圖例表示不同實際路徑；來源裝置按 WHO 第9節文字重構，未按尺寸復刻。</tspan></text>
</g>
</svg>

</details>

<details>
<summary>展開圖 5：公式與誤差判讀總表</summary>

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1900" height="3230" viewBox="0 0 1900 3230" role="img" aria-labelledby="wat3_g5_title wat3_g5_desc">
<title id="wat3_g5_title">水分測定法｜公式與誤差判讀總表</title>
<desc id="wat3_g5_desc">水分測定法v3.0，原生可編輯向量圖。M01先定問題、M02準備與測量、M03讀訊號、M04核對結論。深灰實線為資訊或具條件的因果；紅色虛線為分析者遇到不符時的回查，非分子物理回饋。</desc>
<defs><marker id="wat3_g5_arrow" viewBox="0 0 12 12" refX="10" refY="6" markerWidth="10" markerHeight="10" orient="auto-start-reverse"><path d="M1,1 L11,6 L1,11 Z" fill="#61717a"/></marker><marker id="wat3_g5_red" viewBox="0 0 12 12" refX="10" refY="6" markerWidth="10" markerHeight="10" orient="auto-start-reverse"><path d="M1,1 L11,6 L1,11 Z" fill="#b72d4a"/></marker></defs>
<rect width="1900" height="3230" fill="#f8f6ee"/>
<g font-family="Droid Sans Fallback, Noto Sans CJK TC, PingFang TC, Microsoft JhengHei, sans-serif" fill="#24343f">
<text x="55" y="78" font-size="38" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="55" dy="0">水分測定法｜公式與誤差判讀總表</tspan></text>
<text x="55" y="126" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="55" dy="0">水分測定法 v3.0｜先看用途與條件，再沿入口 → 短鏈 → 出口判讀。</tspan></text>
<text x="55" y="161" font-size="20" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="55" dy="0">KF：費氏水分測定；LOD：乾燥減重；f：mg 水／mL 試劑。灰線資訊向下遊；紅虛線分析者回查。</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="180" width="420" height="77" rx="18" fill="#eeeade" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="75" y="226" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="75" dy="0">M01｜先定問題</tspan></text>
<path d="M475,219 L502,219" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g5_arrow)"/>
<rect x="510" y="180" width="420" height="77" rx="18" fill="#eeeade" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="530" y="226" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="530" dy="0">M02｜準備與測量</tspan></text>
<path d="M930,219 L957,219" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g5_arrow)"/>
<rect x="965" y="180" width="420" height="77" rx="18" fill="#eeeade" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="985" y="226" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="985" dy="0">M03｜讀訊號</tspan></text>
<path d="M1385,219 L1412,219" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g5_arrow)"/>
<rect x="1420" y="180" width="420" height="77" rx="18" fill="#eeeade" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1440" y="226" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1440" dy="0">M04｜核對結論</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="305" width="870" height="330" rx="18" fill="#e8e1f2" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="77" y="345" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="77" dy="0">C01｜問結晶水 mg</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="394.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">m水 = m水合物 × k × M水／M水合物</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="440.95" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">115／230＝0.5 mmol；×2×18＝18 mg。</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="487.75" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">M水＝18 mg/mmol；M水合物＝230 mg/mmol；k＝2；L14。</tspan></text>
<rect x="975" y="305" width="870" height="330" rx="18" fill="#e8e1f2" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="997" y="345" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="997" dy="0">C02｜問力價 f</tspan></text>
<text x="997" y="394.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="997" dy="0">f = 標準水 mg／淨試劑 mL</tspan></text>
<text x="997" y="440.95" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="997" dy="0">18 mg／10.0 mL＝1.80 mg/mL。</tspan></text>
<text x="997" y="487.75" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="997" dy="0">不是把 115 mg 整個鹽當水；L15。</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="690" width="870" height="330" rx="18" fill="#e8e1f2" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="77" y="730" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="77" dy="0">C03｜問容量式水分%</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="779.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">Vnet = Vs − Vb；m水 = fVnet</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="825.9499999999999" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">水分%＝水 mg／原樣 mg ×100%。</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="872.7499999999999" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">Vs 檢品、Vb 空白（mL）；先換回分取倍率；L13、L16。</tspan></text>
<rect x="975" y="690" width="870" height="330" rx="18" fill="#dcecdc" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="997" y="730" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="997" dy="0">C04｜問庫侖式水量</tspan></text>
<text x="997" y="779.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="997" dy="0">m水（g） = Qnet×M水／(2Fc)</tspan></text>
<text x="997" y="825.9499999999999" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="997" dy="0">Fc＝96485 C/mol；M水＝18 g/mol；1.9297 C → 0.18 mg。</tspan></text>
<text x="997" y="872.7499999999999" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="997" dy="0">產碘電量 C；不是終點 mV；有效效率近100%；L12。</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="1075" width="870" height="330" rx="18" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="77" y="1115" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="77" dy="0">C05｜問共沸水分%</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="1164.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">V水淨 = V末 − V初；m水≈ρ水V水淨</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="1210.95" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">水分%＝ρ水×淨水 mL／原樣 g ×100%。</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="1257.75" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">ρ水≈1 g/mL；回收完整，讀下層；L19、L20。</tspan></text>
<rect x="975" y="1075" width="870" height="330" rx="18" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="997" y="1115" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="997" dy="0">C06｜問先取多少樣品</tspan></text>
<text x="997" y="1164.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="997" dy="0">m樣 = ρ水×目標水 mL／水分率</tspan></text>
<text x="997" y="1210.95" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="997" dy="0">4%＝0.04；要 2 mL，取樣≈2／0.04＝50 g。</tspan></text>
<text x="997" y="1257.75" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="997" dy="0">理想回收估算，須再核方法／代表性；L03、L20。</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="1460" width="870" height="330" rx="18" fill="#f5dfe2" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="77" y="1500" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="77" dy="0">C07｜問乾燥減重%</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="1549.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">LOD% = (m₁−m₂)／(m₁−m₀) ×100%</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="1595.95" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">m₀空瓶；m₁瓶＋原樣；m₂瓶＋乾樣。</tspan></text>
<text x="77" y="1642.75" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="77" dy="0">除原樣重；不直接把全部損失稱水；L01、L21。</tspan></text>
<rect x="975" y="1460" width="870" height="330" rx="18" fill="#f5dfe2" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="997" y="1500" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="997" dy="0">C08｜問允許稱量差</tspan></text>
<text x="997" y="1549.15" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="997" dy="0">允許差 = 明定比例 × 明定基準質量</tspan></text>
<text x="997" y="1595.95" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="997" dy="0">自編：10 g×0.25%＝25 mg。</tspan></text>
<text x="997" y="1642.75" font-size="24" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="997" dy="0">分母必須題目／方法明示；0.5 mg/g另論；L22。</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="1875" width="870" height="585" rx="18" fill="white" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="2"/>
<rect x="975" y="1875" width="870" height="585" rx="18" fill="white" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="80" y="1920" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="80" dy="0">固定電壓 → 讀 I_det 上升（雙安培）</tspan></text>
<text x="1000" y="1920" font-size="27" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="1000" dy="0">固定小電流 → 讀 U 下降（雙電位）</tspan></text>
<text x="80" y="1965" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="80" dy="0">自編示意 8→80 µA；題庫範圍 5–10→50–150 µA。</tspan></text>
<text x="1000" y="1965" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1000" dy="0">U：指示電極間電壓（mV）；不指定實際數值。</tspan></text>
<path d="M160,2020 L160,2330 L870,2330" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g5_arrow)"/>
<path d="M150,2330 L160,2330" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<text x="90" y="2337" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="90" dy="0">0</tspan></text>
<path d="M150,2230 L160,2230" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<text x="90" y="2237" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="90" dy="0">50</tspan></text>
<path d="M150,2130 L160,2130" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<text x="90" y="2137" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="90" dy="0">100</tspan></text>
<path d="M150,2030 L160,2030" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<text x="90" y="2037" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="90" dy="0">150</tspan></text>
<text x="170" y="2045" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="170" dy="0">I_det（µA）</tspan></text>
<path d="M180,2314 L525,2314 L585,2170 L840,2170" fill="none" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M550,2020 L550,2330" fill="none" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-dasharray="10 8"/>
<text x="564" y="2060" font-size="21" fill="#685185" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="564" dy="0">終點示意</tspan></text>
<text x="195" y="2378" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="195" dy="0">試劑加入量（mL，示意）→</tspan></text>
<path d="M1080,2020 L1080,2330 L1790,2330" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" marker-end="url(#wat3_g5_arrow)"/>
<text x="1000" y="2053" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1000" dy="0">較高</tspan></text>
<text x="1000" y="2295" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1000" dy="0">較低</tspan></text>
<path d="M1100,2070 L1410,2070 L1470,2280 L1760,2280" fill="none" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<path d="M1440,2020 L1440,2330" fill="none" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-dasharray="10 8"/>
<text x="1460" y="2120" font-size="21" fill="#685185" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1460" dy="0">終點示意</tspan></text>
<text x="1110" y="2378" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1110" dy="0">供碘程式（示意）→</tspan></text>
<text x="80" y="2420" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="80" dy="0">L10：碘／碘離子可互轉 → 電流較大。</tspan></text>
<text x="1000" y="2420" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="1000" dy="0">L11：相同指示電流較容易維持 → 電壓較低。</tspan></text>
<rect x="55" y="2520" width="1790" height="590" rx="18" fill="#eeeade" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="80" y="2565" font-size="28" fill="#24343f" font-weight="bold"><tspan x="80" dy="0">看見偏差 → 先列候選 → 以證據區分 → 回正確站</tspan></text>
<rect x="80" y="2600" width="1735" height="66" rx="10" fill="white" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="100" y="2640" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="100" dy="0">KF 空白／漂移未扣</tspan></text>
<text x="620" y="2640" font-size="23" fill="#b72d4a" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="620" dy="0">通常偏高</tspan></text>
<text x="960" y="2628" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="960" dy="0">M02 查密封與背景；M03 查是否已校正；L13。</tspan></text>
<rect x="80" y="2674" width="1735" height="66" rx="10" fill="white" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="100" y="2714" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="100" dy="0">固定 V 下，使用的 f 過大</tspan></text>
<text x="620" y="2714" font-size="23" fill="#b72d4a" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="620" dy="0">算出的水量偏高</tspan></text>
<text x="960" y="2702" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="960" dy="0">M02 重標定；M03 核對輸入 f；L15、L23。</tspan></text>
<rect x="80" y="2748" width="1735" height="66" rx="10" fill="white" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="100" y="2788" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="100" dy="0">KF 萃取／結晶水釋出不全</tspan></text>
<text x="620" y="2788" font-size="23" fill="#b72d4a" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="620" dy="0">通常偏低</tspan></text>
<text x="960" y="2776" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="960" dy="0">M02 查溶解／釋水與回收，不能只說樣品很乾；L08。</tspan></text>
<rect x="80" y="2822" width="1735" height="66" rx="10" fill="white" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="100" y="2862" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="100" dy="0">蒸餾水滴留壁、溶劑留水</tspan></text>
<text x="620" y="2862" font-size="23" fill="#b72d4a" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="620" dy="0">收集體積偏低</tspan></text>
<text x="960" y="2850" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="960" dy="0">M02 查飽和／移下水滴／回收；M03 重讀下層；L19。</tspan></text>
<rect x="80" y="2896" width="1735" height="66" rx="10" fill="white" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="100" y="2936" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="100" dy="0">LOD 同時失去其他揮發物</tspan></text>
<text x="620" y="2936" font-size="23" fill="#b72d4a" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="620" dy="0">相對真水量可能偏高</tspan></text>
<text x="960" y="2924" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="960" dy="0">M01／M04 核對要報 LOD 還是水；必要時換方法；L01。</tspan></text>
<rect x="80" y="2970" width="1735" height="66" rx="10" fill="white" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="100" y="3010" font-size="23" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="100" dy="0">乾燥後再吸濕</tspan></text>
<text x="620" y="3010" font-size="23" fill="#b72d4a" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="620" dy="0">乾重偏高、LOD 偏低</tspan></text>
<text x="960" y="2998" font-size="22" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="960" dy="0">M02 修正乾燥器冷卻與稱量，重新取有效讀值；L22。</tspan></text>
<text x="55" y="3156" font-size="21" fill="#24343f" font-weight="normal"><tspan x="55" dy="0">L23：偏高／偏低不能唯一反推原因。紫虛線是終點位置示意；品質回查以文字指定 M 站與修正動作。</tspan></text>
</g>
</svg>

</details>

圖中使用可編輯的原生 `<text>`／`<tspan>`；內嵌內容與五個獨立 SVG 同源。已以 Inkscape 渲染檢視；各閱讀器對原生 SVG 的顯示支援仍有差異，可開同資料夾中的獨立檔。
